PRELIMINARY REMARKS. 


IN the preparation of this volume, containing the CATUKKA 
NrPàTA, I have had the use of three additional Manuscripts, 
namely, B. K. 122, B. K. 123 (Burmese writing), from 
the King's Library in the India Office Library, and S. M. 
(Siühalese character), a very accurate manuscript on palm 
leaves in my own collection. The Burmese texts are 
copies of the same original which have no variant readings 
of any consequence, and are always cited as B. K. 

In the Siühalese manuscripts of the Aügutiara there are 
no titles of vaggas nor uddānas after the tenth vagga 
(dutiya paññasaka). The omission has been supplied by 
the Burmese copies. The Commentary has titles of vaggas 
agreeing with those in the Burmese manuscripts, except 
that it has Kosiya vagga for Kesi vagga (No. 12) 
and Bràhmana vagga for Yodhājīva vagga 
(No. 19). 

We again gel a tag at the end of the last vagga, as in 
the third nipata (Anguttara pt. 1. p. 168), which is repeated 
at the end of the final vagga in all the remaining nipātas 
(v-x1). 

I have preferred in most cases the Siühalese to the 
Burmese readings. In some instances perhaps both are 
wrong. On p. 214 (Sutta 200, $8 9. 17) for the Siühalese 
leetion apajjhāyati and the Burmese sampajjhā- 
yati we ought to read pajjhāyati, the explanation 
given in the Commentary (see Jàt. III. p. 584, where 
pajjhāyati means ‘to be consumed with grief"). Apaj- 
jhāyati, from the root dhyā, usually signifies to 
muse, consider, though in Sanskrit it bas quite the 


4 PRELIMINARY REMARKS. 


opposite meaning. In the Majjhima-Nikaya 50, p. 334, we 
find jhāyati, pajjhāyati, nijjhāyati, apaj- 
jhāyati used with probably little variation of meaning. 
There may have arisen, perhaps, some confusion of mean- 
ing between the prepositional compounds of the two roots 
dhyā and kshā, which have only one form in Pāli. 

Our text (iv. 199, p. 214) enables us to correct a slight 
error in 8 parallel phrase in the Dīpavamsa XII. 32, p. 64, 
**[Tambapanni] tanta kulakajātā gulāgunthikajātā 
muūjababbajabhūtā” translated by Dr. Oldenberg 
as. follows :—“ It [Tambapanni] is exhausted ; high-born 
people have become (as it were) people covered with sores, 
and have become (feeble like) Muiija or Babbaja grass.” 
The Anguttara has “ayam loko .. . tantakulakajato 
gulagundikajato (v. 1. gulāgunthikajāto) muūjababbajab- 
hūto.” All the epithets in the Dipavamsa belong to 
Tambapanni and involve a plant-metaphor. 

We must read tantākulakajāto (=tanta-āku- 
lakajāto), that is, tantam viya ākulajāto= 
entangled, matted together; guļā or gulā cannot mean 
here sores or pimples, but probably knobs; gundika or 
gunthika may signify excrescences, and gulagundi- 
kajāto, blighted. 

There is a passage in the Dhammapada commentary 
(p. 209), not noticed by Childers, in which gandikaj ata: 
(?— gundikajata) is applied to flowers : 

“ Yathà nama kusalo mālākāro pupphārāmam pavisitvā 
tarunamakulāni ca pànakaviddhàni ca militàni ca gandika- 
jātāni ca pupphāni vajjetvā sobhanāni sujatàn' eva pup- 
phani cināti,” &c. 

I have added indexes of ** Subjects and Words," “ Proper 
Names," and “ Gāthās,” as in Part I. 

I take this opportunity of expressing my best thanks to Dr. 
Rost for the loan of the fine MSS. from the King's Collec- 
tion, and I have again to express my great indebtedness to 
Dr. Trenckner for the long loan of his accurate transcript 
of the Copenhagen Manuscript.

Cattàro 'me bhikkhave tanhuppādā yattha bhikkhuno 
tanhà uppajjamānā uppajjati. Katame cattāro ? 

Civarahetu và bhikkhave bhikkhuno tanhà uppajjamānā 
uppajjati, pindapatahetu vā... senāsanahetu vā... 
itibhavābhavahetu và bhikkhave bhikkhuno tanhà uppaj- 
jamanā uppajjati.

tanhā uppajjamànà uppajjatiti.

Cattar' imāni bhikkhave padhānāni. Katamāni cattāri ? 

Samvarappadhānam, pahānappadhānam, bhāvanappa- 
dhānam, anurakkhanappadhānam ? 

Katamaii ca bhikkhave samvarappadhànam ? 

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno cakkhunā disvà na nimittag- 
gāhī hoti nānuvyaūjanaggāhī hoti yatvádhikaranam enam 
cakkhundriyam asamvutam viharantam abhijhádomanassa `

patipajjati rakkhati cakkhundriyam cakkhundriye samvaram 
āpajjati ; sotena saddam sutvā ... pe... ghānena 
gandham ghàyitvà . . . pe... jivhaya rasam sāyitvā 
e... pe... kāyena photthabbam phusitvà . .. pe... 
manasā dhammam viññaya na nimittaggāhī hoti nānuvyaū- 
janaggahi yatvadhikaranam enam manindriyam asamvutam 
viharantam abhijhadomanassà pāpakā akusalà dhammā 
anvassaveyyum : tassa samvārāya patipajjati rakkhati ma- 
nindriyam manindriye samvaram āpajjati. Idam vuccati 
bhikkhave samvarappadhānam. 

Katamaī ca bhikkhave pahānappadhānam ? 

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu uppannam kāmavitakkam nā- 
dhivāseti pajahati vinodeti vyantikaroti anabhāvam gameti, 
uppannam vyāpādavitakkam . . . pe... , uppannam 
vihimsāvitakkam ... pe... uppannuppanne pāpake 
akusale dhamme nādhivāseti pajahati vinodeti vyantikaroti 
anabhāvam gameti. Idam vuccati bhikkhave pahāna- 
ppadhānam.

yathābhatam nikkhitto evam sagge. Katamehi catuhi ?

Imehi kho bhikkhave catuhi dhammehi samannāgato 
bhattuddesako yathābhatam nikkhitto evam sagge ti.

Na chandā dosā na bhayā ca gāmino 
Parisāya mando ca pan” esa vuccati 
Evam hi vuttam samano na jānatāti.

abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavanno kevalakappam Jeta- 
vanam obhāsetvā yen’ āham ten’ upasankami, upasanka- 
mitvā mam abhivādetvā ekamantam atthāsi. Ekamaniam 
thito kho bhikkhave Rohitasso devaputto mam etad 
avoca :—Yattha nu kho na jayati . . . na uppajjati, sakka 
nu kho so: bhante gamanena lokassa antam ñatum và 
datthum và pàpunitum vāti ? 

Evam vutte aham bhikkhave Rohitassam devaputtam 
etad avoca :—Yattha kho āvuso na jāyati . . . na uppajjati 
nāham tam gamanena lokassa antam itātayyam datthay- 
yam pattayyanti vadamiti. 

' Evam vutte bhikkhave Rohitasso devaputto mam etad 
avoca :—Acchariyam bhante abbhutam bhante yàva subhā- 
sitam e' idam bhante Bhagavatā—yattha kho àvuso? na 
jāyati . . . na uppajati nàham tam gamanena . . . pattay- 
yan ti vadāmi.

bhojaputto iddhimā vehāsaūgamo. Tassa mayham bhante 
evarūpo javo ahosi seyyathāpi nāma daļhadhammo dhanug- 
gaho sikkhito katahattho katupāsano lahukena asanena 
appakasirena tiriyam tālacehātīm atipāteyya,* evarūpo 
padavitiharo ahosi seyyathāpi nāma puratthimā samuddā 
paechimo samuddo. Tassa mayham bhante evarūpena 
javena samannāgatassa evarūpena ca padavītihārena evarū- 
pam icehāgatam uppajji —aham gamanena lokass' antam 
papunissàmiti. So kho 'ham bhante aññatr'eva asitapita-

8. Katamañ ca gahapati atthisukham ? 

Idha gahapati kulaputtassa bhogā honti utthānaviriyā- 
dhigatā bāhābalaparicitā sedāvakkhittā dhammika dham- 
maladdhā, so—bhogā me atthi utthānaviriyādhigatā . . . 
dhammaladdhā ti —adhigacchati sukham  adhigaechati 
somanassam. Idam vuccati gahapati atthisukham. 

4. Katamañ ca gahapati bhogasukham ? 

Idha gahapati kulaputto utthānaviriyādhigatehi bhogehi 
bāhābalaparicitehi sedāvakkhittehi dhammikehi dhamma- 
laddhehi bhoge ca bhuiijati puññani ca karoti, so —utthàna- 
viriyādhigatehi bhogehi . . . dhammaladdhehi bhoge ca, 
bhuiijmi puññani ca karomiti—adhigacchati sukham 
adhigaechati somanassam. Idam assa vuccati gahapati 
bhogasukham. | 

5. Katamañ ca gahapati anana?-sukham ? Idha gahapati 
kulaputto na kassa kiñei dhāreti 3 appam và bahum và, so— 
na kassa kinei dharemi4 appam và bahum và ti—adhigac- 
chati sukham adhigaechati somanassam. Idam vuccati 
gahapati ananasukham. 

6. Katamaii ca gahapati anavajja-sukham ? 

Idha gahapati ariyasāvako anavajjena kayakammena sa- 
mannāgato hoti anavajjena vacikammena samannāgato 
hoti anavajjena manokammena samannāgato hoti, so—

dahati. Idam vuccati bhikkhave pahānappadhānam. `

dhammànam uppādāya chandam janeti . . . padahati.

mānam thitiyà asammosaya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhā-

ārabhati cittam pagganhāti padahati. Idam vuccati 
bhikkhave anurakkhanappadhānam.

lokasmin ti. 


99. 


1. Cattāro 'me bhikkhave puggalà santo samvijjamanā 
lokasmim. Katame cattāro? Attahitāya patipanno hoti

no parahitāya, parahitàya patipanno hoti no attahitāya, 
n'ev attahitāya patipanno hoti no parahitāya, attahitàya 
ca patipanno hoti parahitāya ca. 

2. Kathaii ca bhikkhave puggalo attahitāya patipanno 
hoti no parahitāya ? | 

Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo attanà pānātipātā pati- 
virato hoti no param pānātipātā veramaniyà samādapeti, 
attanā adinnādānā pativirato hoti no param adinnādānā 
veramaniyà samādapeti, attanà kamesu micchācārā pati- 
virato hoti . . . samādapeti, attanà musāvādā pativirato 
hoti . . . samàdapeti, attanà  suramerayamajjapama- 
- datthānā pativirato hoti . . . samādapeti. Evam kho 
bhikkhave puggalo attahitāya patipanno hoti no para- 
hitāya. 

8. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo parahitāya patipanno 
hoti no attahitāya ? 

Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo attanā pānātipātā apati- 
virato hoti param panàtipatà veramaniyà samadapeti, attanā 
adīnnādānā .. . pe... attanā musàvàdà . . . pe... 
attanā surāmerayamajjapamādatthānā . . . pe... Evam 
kho bhikkhave puggalo parahitāya patipanno hoti no atta- 
hitāya. 

4. Kathaii ca bhikkhave puggalo n'ev' attahitāya pati- 
panno hoti no parahitāya ? 

Idha bhikkhave ekaeco puggalo attanà pānātipātā apati- 
virato hoti no param pānātipātā veramaniyā samādapeti 
...pe... surāmerayamajjapamādatthānā veramaniyā 
samādapeti. Evam kho bhikkhave puggalo n'ev' attahitāya 
patipanno hoti no parahitàya. 

5. Kathañ ca bhikkhave puggalo attahitaya ca patipanno 
hoti parahitàya ca ? 

Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo attanā ca pānātipātā 
pativirato hoti parañ ca pānātipātā veramaniyà samādapeti 
...pe... suraàmerayamajjapamadatthana veramaniyà 
samádapeti. Evam kho bhikkhave puggalo attahitàya ca 
patipanno hoti parahitāya ca. Ime kho bhikkhave cattāro 
puggalā santo samvijjamānā lokasmin ti.

1. Evam me sutam. Ekam samayam Bhagavā Savatthi-

2. Cattāro "me bhikkhave valāhakā. Katame cattaro ? 

Gajjitā no vassità, vassitā no gajjitā, n' eva gajjità no 
vassitā, gajjitā ca vassitā ca. 

Ime kho bhikkhave cattāro valāhakā. 

9. Evam eva kho bhikkhave cattāro "me valāhakūpamā 
puggalā santo samvijjamānā lokasmim.  Katame cattāro ? 

Gajjitā no vassitā, vassità no gajjitā, n' eva gajjitā no 
vassitā, gajjitā ca vassitā ca. 

4. Kathaū ca bhikkhave puggalo gajjitā hoti no vassitā ? 
Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo bhāsitā hoti no katta. Evam 
kho bhikkhave puggalo gajjità hoti no vassitā, seyyathāpi 
80 bhikkhave valāhako gajjitā no vassitā, tathūpamāham 
bhikkhave imam puggalam vadāmi. 

5. Kathaū ca bhikkhave puggalo vassitā hoti no gajjità ? 

Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo katta hoti no bhāsitā. 
Evam kho bhikkhave puggalo vassitā hotino gajjitā, sey- 
yathāpi so bhikkhave valāhako vassità no gajjità, tathūpa- 
māham bhikkhave imam puggalam vadāmi. 

6. Kathaii ca bhikkhave puggalo n'eva gajjità no vassitā ? 

Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo n'eva bhāsitā hoti no 
katta. Evam kho bhikkhave puggalo n'eva gajjita hoti no 
vassitā, seyyathāpi so bhikkhave valāhako n'eva gajjitā no 
vassitā, tathūpamāham bhikkhave imam puggalam vadāmi. 

7. Kathaū ca bhikkhave puggalo gajjità ca hoti vassitā 
ca ? 

Idha bhikkhave ekacco puggalo bhāsitā ca hoti katta ca. 
Evam kho bhikkhave puggalo gajjitā ca hoti vassitā ca, 
seyyathāpi so bhikkhave valāhako gajjità ca hoti vassitā ca, 
tathüpamaham bhikkhave imam puggalam vadāmi. Ime 
kho bhikkhave cattāro valāhakūpamā puggalā santo sam- 
vijjāamānā lokasmin ti.

abhikkantam patikkantam ālokitam vilokitam sammifijitam

nirodhagàmini patipada ti yathābhūtam nappajānāti. Evam

bhikkhave kumbho tuecho pihito, tathūpamāham bhik- 
khave imam puggalam vadàmi.

ldha bhikkhave ekaccassa puggalassa na pāsādikam hoti 
abhikkantam ... sanghātipattacīvaradhāranam. So idam

dhagàmini patipada ti yathābhūtam pajānāti. Evam kho 
bhikkhave puggalo püro hoti vivato, seyyathàpi so bhik- 
khave kumbho pūro vivato, tathüpamaham bhikkhave imam

Idha bhikkhave ekaecassa puggalassa na pāsādikam hoti 
abhikkantam ... saūghātipattacīvaradhāranam. So idam 
dukkhan ti yathābhūtam nappajānāti . . . ayam dukkha- 
nirodhagāminī patipadā ti yathābhūtam nappajanati. Evam 
kho bhikkhave puggalo tuccho hoti vivato, seyyathāpi so 
bhikkhave kumbho tuceho vivato, tathūpamāham bhikkhave 
imam puggalam vadāmi.

abhikkantam . . . sanghātipattacīvaradhāranņam. So idam

116. 


1. Catuhi bhikkhave thānehi appamādo  karaniyo. 
Katamehi catuhi ? 

Kāyaduccaritam bhikkhave pajahatha kāyasucaritam 
bhāvetha tattha ca mā pamādattha.  Vacīduccaritam 
bhikkhave pajahatha vacisucaritam bhāvetha tattha ca mā 
pamadattha. Manoduccaritam bhikkhave pajahatha mano- 
sucaritam bhāvetha tattha ca mā pamādattha. Micehā- 
ditthim bhikkhave pajahatha sammāditthim bhāvetha 
tattha ca mā pamādattha.

yesu dhammesu cittam dussīti attarūpena appamādo sati 
cetaso ārakkho karanīyo. Mà me mohaniyesu dhammesu 
cittam muyhiti attarüpena appamādo sati cetaso ārakkho 
karaniy.. Mā me madaniyesu dhammesu cittam majjiti 
attarūpena appamado sati cetaso ārakkho karaniyo.

pi gacchatīti.

chānayonim pi gaechati pittivisayam pi gaechati. Bhaga- 
vato pana sāvako tattha yāvatāyukam thatvā yāvatakam 
tesam devānam āyuppamānam tam sabbam khepetvā 
tasmim yeva bhave parinibbàyati. Ayam- kho bhikkhave 
viseso ayam adhippāyāso idam mānākaranam sutavato 
ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthujjanena yadidam gatiyā 
uppattiyà sati. |

mosāya antaradhānāya samvattanti. Katame cattāro ? : 

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu duggahitam suttantam pariyā- 
punanti dunnikkhittehi padavyaūjanehi. Dunnikkhittassa 
bhikkhave padavyainjanassa attho pi dunnnayo hoti. Ayam 
bhikkhave pathamo dhammo sammosāva antaradhānāya 
samvattati.2 

4, Puna ca param bhikkhave bhikkhū dubbacā honti 
dovacassakaraņehi dhammehi samannāgatā akkhamā ap- 
padakkhiņaggāhino anusāsanim. Ayam bhikkhave dutiyo 
dhammo sammosāya antaradhānāya samvattati.

āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā te na 
sakkaceam suttantam param vācenti, tesam aecayena chin- 
namülako suttanto hoti appatisarano. Ayam bhikkhave 
tatiyo dhammo sammosāya antaradhānāya samvattati. |

bhiūiā?

kham domanassam patisamvedeti, pakatiya pi tibbamoha- 
jātiko hoti abhikkhanam mohajam dukkham domanassam

bhavanti — saddhindriyam viriyindriyjam  satindriyam

indriypanam muduttā dandham ānantariyam 3 pāpunāti 
āsavānam khayāya. Ayam vuccati bhikkhave dukkhā pati- 
pada dandhābiūiā.

cetanā kamati no parasaīcetanā.

tāni pidaheyya yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni vivareyya devo 
ca na sammādhāram anupaveccheyya, evam hi tassā bhik- 
khave jambāliyā na ālippabhedo 2 pātikankho, evam eva kho 
bhikkhave bhikkhuno aiūiūataram santam cetovimuttam 
upasampajja viharati. So avijjāpabhedam manasikaroti, 
tassa avijjāpabhedam manasikaroto avijjāpabhede cittam 
nappakkhandati . . . tassa kho etam bhikkhave bhikkhuno 
na avijjāpabhedo pātikankho. 

4. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhu aññataram santam 
cetovimuttim upasampajja viharati So avijjāpabhedam 
manasikaroti, tassa avijjāpabhedam manasīkaroto avij- 
jāpabhede cittam ^ pakkhandati pasīdati santitthati 
vimuceati, tassa kho etam bhikkhave bhikkhuno avijjā- 
pabhedo pātikankho. ^ Sevyathápi bhikkhave jambālī 
auekavassaganikā tassā puriso yāni ¢ eva āyamukhāni 
tani vivareyya yāni apāyamukhāni tani pidaheyya devo ca 
sammādhāram anupaveccheyya, evam hi tassā bhikkhave 
bhikkhuno aññataram santam cetovimuttim upasampajja 
viharati. So avijjāpabhedam manasikaroti, tassa avij-

earantiti, so tesu pemam janeti. Evam kho bhikkhave pemā

kappo hoti parañ ca sammāsankappe samādapeti, attanā ca» 
sammāvāco hoti paraii ca sammāvācāya samādapeti, attanā. 
ca sammākammanto hoti parañ ca sammākammante 
samādapeti, attanā ca sammā-ājīvo hoti parañ ca sammā- 
àjive samādapeti, attanā ca sammāvāyāmo hoti parañ ca 
sammāvāyāme samādapeti, attanā ca sammāsati hoti parañ 
ca sammāsatiyā samādapeti, attanā ca sammāsamādhi hoti 
paraii ca sammāsamādhimhi samādapeti. 

Ayam vuccati bhikkhave sappurisena sappurisataro.

2. Katamañ ca bhikkhave kammam kanham kanhavipā- 
kam ? 

Idha : bhikkave ekacco savyāpajjham kāyasankhāram 
abhisankharoti. . . savyāpajjham manosankharam abhisan- 
kharoti. So savyāpajjham kāyasankhāram abhisankharitvā 
savyāpajjham vacīsankhāram abhisankharitvā savyāpaj- 
jham manosankhāram abhisankharitvā savyapajjham lokam 
uppajjati. Tam enam savyāpajjham lokam uppannam 
samānam savyāpajjhā phassa phusanti. So savyāpajjhehi 
phassehi phuttho samāno savyapajjham vedanam vediyati 
ekantadukkham, seyyathāpi sattā nerayika. Idam vuccati 
bhikkhave kammam kanham kanhavipakam.

sankharitvà . . . pe. . . manosankhāram abhisanharitvā 
avyāpajjham lokam uppajjati. Tam enam avyāpajjham 
lokam uppannam samānam avyāpajjhā phassā phusanti.

vedanam vediyati ekantasukham, seyyathāpi devā Subha- 
kiņhā.

1. Atha kho Sikho Moggallāno brahmano yena Bhagavā

paūūattā appasannānam pasādāya pasannānam bhiyyo- 
bhavāya, yathā yathā .bhikkhave mayā sāvakānam abhi- 
samācārikā sikkhā paññatta appasannānam pasādāya pa- 
sannānam bhiyyobhāvāya tatha tathā so tassā sikkhāya 
akkhandakārī hoti aechiddakàri asabalakari akammāsakārī 
samādāya sikkhati sikkhapadesu. 

Puna ca param bhikkhave mayā sāvakānam ādibrahma- 
cariyikā sikkhā paññatta sabbaso sammādukkhakkhayāya, 
yathā yathā bhikkhave mayā sāvakānam . . . sabbaso 
sammādukkhakkhayāya tathà tathā so tassā sikkhaya ak- 
khaņdakārī hoti acchiddakārī . . . sikkhapadesu. Evam 
kho bhikkhave sikkhānisamsam hoti. 

2. Kathai ca bhikkhave paññuttaram hoti ? 

Idha bhikkhave mayā savakanam dhammā desitā sabbaso 
sammādukkhakkhayāya, yathà yathà bhikkhave mayā 
sāvakānam . . . sabbaso sammādukkhakkhayāya tathà 
tathā 'ssa 3 te dhammā paiūūāya samavekkhita honti. 
Evam kho bhikkhave paūiūuttaram hoti.

sāmi! paripüram và abhisamācārikam sikkham tattha

sikkham paripūressāmi paripūram vā ādibrahmacariyikam 
sikkham tattha tattha paññaya anuggahessāmīti ajjhattam 
yeva sati sūpatthitā hoti. Iti asamavekkhitam vā dham- 
mam paūūāya samavekkhissāmi samavekkhitam vā dham- 
mam tattha tattha paññaya anuggahessāmīti ajjhattam 
yeva sati sūpatthitā hoti. Iti aphassitam 2 vā dhammam 
vimuttiyā phassissāmi, phassitam vā dhammam tattha 
tattha paññaya anuggahessāmīti ajjhattam yeva sati sū- 
patthitā hoti. Evam kho bhikkhave satādhipateyyam 


hoti.

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiūūāya cattāro dhammā bhāve- 
tabbā. Katame cattāro ? 

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī

nāsu... citte . . . dhammesu dhammanupassi viharati 
ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhàdomanassam.?

bhāvetabbā ti. 

2. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññaya cattāro dhammā bhāve- 
tabbā. Katame cattāro ?

akusalānam dhammānam anuppādāya chandam  janeti 
vayamati viriyam ārabhati cittam pagganhāti padahati,

nàyà ... pe... , anuppannànam kusalānam dhammā- 
nam  uppadaya ... pe ... uppannanam kusalānam 
dhammānam thitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya 
cittam pagganhāti padahati. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiüiüaya 
ime cattāro dhammā bhāvetabbā. 

9. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiūiāya cattāro dhammā bhāve- 
tabbā. Katame cattāro?

pe..., cittasamādhi .. . pe... , vīmamsāsamādhi-

bhāvetabbā.
